# A/B trial measurements, arm A2 -- run log

> Append-only. One row per run of `evals/ab/run-trial.ts` for arm A2 (`workdoo-a2`, `clinic-a2`; schedule
> `evals/ab/schedule-a2.json`), written by the harness, never by hand. Same row format and rules as the S0 log
> (`ab-measurements.md`, closed), which keeps A0 and A1. Tokens and cost come from the CLI's own `result` event.
> A `usage-missing` row is invalid: the CLI reported no usage, and the trial stops for an owner ruling.
>
> **Pre-registration frozen 2026-11-02T00:40:46Z (owner-signed, SPRINT-154 T1, Amendment 6):** `evals/ab/PREREGISTRATION.md` sha256
> `f6b770f1c0a1210b66620f27b5f22bab9cc405c959cb67425b2ddd17aa7b470f`, at commit `e6b621bd`. It carries over nothing: every A2
> row must postdate this instant. A2 is judged by Amendment 6 (validity on its own 32 slots first, then the ruling against A1).

{"tool":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\HYPEAM~1\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\workdoo-seat-check\\check.mjs","content":"import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\nimport { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';\nimport { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\nconst repo = process.argv[2];\nconst { PrivateClient, privateWorkspaceHarness } = await import(pathToFileURL(`${repo}/scripts/lib/private-workspace-harness.mjs`).href);\nconst harness = await privateWorkspaceHarness('workdoo-seat-');\nlet checks = 0;\nconst eq = (a, b, label) => { assert.deepEqual(a, b, label); checks++; console.log('ok -', label); };\ntry {\n  await harness.start();\n  const owner = new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await owner.signUp('Seat Owner', 'owner@seat.example');\n  const ws = (await owner.request('POST', '/api/workspaces', { name: 'Seat Co' })).data.workspace;\n  const base = `/api/workspaces/${ws.id}`;\n  const invite = email => owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations`, { email });\n  const op = new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await op.signUp('Seat Operator', 'op@seat.example');\n  const first = await invite('op@seat.example');\n  eq(first.status, 201, 'owner invites first operator');\n  const token = new URL(first.data.invitePath, harness.origin).hash.replace(/^#invite=/, '');\n  eq((await op.request('POST', '/api/invitations/accept', { token })).status, 200, 'operator accepts');\n  const pending = [];\n  for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) { const r = await invite(`p${i}@seat.example`); eq(r.status, 201, `pending invitation ${i} uses seat ${i + 1} (owner not counted)`); pending.push(r.data.invitation); }\n  const over = await invite('over@seat.example');\n  eq(over.status, 409, '6th seat refused with 409');\n  eq(over.data.error.code, 'OPERATOR_SEAT_LIMIT', 'seat limit code');\n  console.log('   message:', over.data.error.message);\n  eq((await invite('p1@seat.example')).data.error.code, 'INVITATION_PENDING', 'duplicate pending email keeps INVITATION_PENDING');\n  eq((await invite('op@seat.example')).data.error.code, 'ALREADY_MEMBER', 'existing member keeps ALREADY_MEMBER');\n  // Revoke frees a seat.\n  eq((await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${pending[0].id}/revoke`, {})).status, 200, 'revoke pending invitation');\n  eq((await invite('after-revoke@seat.example')).status, 201, 'revoked invitation returns its seat');\n  eq((await invite('still-full@seat.example')).data.error.code, 'OPERATOR_SEAT_LIMIT', 'full again after reuse');\n  // Expiry frees a seat.\n  const db = new DatabaseSync(harness.database, { timeout: 1000 });\n  db.prepare('UPDATE organization_invitations SET expires_at=? WHERE id=?').run('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z', pending[1].id);\n  db.close();\n  eq((await invite('after-expiry@seat.example')).status, 201, 'expired invitation returns its seat');\n  eq((await invite('still-full-2@seat.example')).data.error.code, 'OPERATOR_SEAT_LIMIT', 'full again after expiry reuse');\n  // Disabling the operator frees a seat.\n  const roster = (await owner.request('GET', `${base}/people`)).data.members;\n  const opMember = roster.find(m => m.role === 'operator');\n  const disabled = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/access/commands`, { commandId: randomUUID(), type: 'change_access', membershipId: opMember.id, expectedRevision: 1, role: 'operator', status: 'disabled' });\n  eq(disabled.status, 200, 'owner disables operator access');\n  eq((await invite('after-disable@seat.example')).status, 201, 'disabled operator returns their seat');\n  eq((await invite('still-full-3@seat.example')).data.error.code, 'OPERATOR_SEAT_LIMIT', 'full again after disable reuse');\n  eq((await invite('op@seat.example')).data.error.code, 'MEMBERSHIP_DISABLED', 'disabled member keeps MEMBERSHIP_DISABLED');\n  console.log(`Seat check: ${checks} checks passed.`);\n} finally { await harness.close(); }\n"}} | {"graders":[{"name":"typecheck","result":"not-run"},{"name":"build","result":"not-run"},{"name":"verify:workspace:access:api","result":"not-run"},{"name":"lint","result":"not-run"}],"judges":{"convention":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\workdoo-seat-check\\\\check.mjs\",\"content\":\"import assert from 'n"},"business":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\workdoo-seat-check\\\\check.mjs\",\"content\":\"import assert from 'n"},"scope":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\workdoo-seat-check\\\\check.mjs\",\"content\":\"import assert from 'n"}},"exec":{"input":142,"output":37216,"cacheRead":4912599,"cacheWrite":228357,"costUsd":3.5542638,"subagents":0,"models":["claude-opus-5-5"]},"setup":null,"judgeOverhead":{"input":2,"output":108,"cacheRead":649,"cacheWrite":3469,"costUsd":0.015024899999999999},"attemptKind":"scored","attemptClass":"task-failure","setupWallS":20.155} |

new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await owner.signUp('Avery Owner', 'avery@reissue.example');\n  const ws = (await owner.request('POST', '/api/workspaces', { name: 'Reissue Co' })).data.workspace;\n  const base = `/api/workspaces/${ws.id}`;\n  const blake = new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await blake.signUp('Blake Invitee', 'blake@reissue.example');\n\n  // pending -> reissue\n  const first = (await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations`, { email: 'blake@reissue.example' })).data;\n  const oldToken = tokenOf(first.invitePath);\n  const r1 = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${first.invitation.id}/reissue`, {});\n  eq(r1.status, 201, 'Pending invitation reissues with 201');\n  eq(r1.data.invitation.email, 'blake@reissue.example', 'Reissue keeps the same email');\n  eq(r1.data.invitation.status, 'pending', 'Reissued invitation is pending');\n  eq(r1.data.invitation.id !== first.invitation.id, true, 'Reissue creates a new invitation id');\n  const newToken = tokenOf(r1.data.invitePath);\n  eq(newToken !== oldToken, true, 'Reissue issues a fresh token');\n  eq((await blake.request('POST', '/api/invitations/inspect', { token: oldToken })).status, 404, 'Old link stops working');\n  eq((await blake.request('POST', '/api/invitations/inspect', { token: newToken })).status, 200, 'New link works');\n  const list = (await owner.request('GET', `${base}/invitations`)).data.invitations;\n  eq(list.find(i => i.id === first.invitation.id).status, 'revoked', 'Original pending invitation is now revoked');\n\n  // revoked -> reissue\n  await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${r1.data.invitation.id}/revoke`, {});\n  const r2 = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${r1.data.invitation.id}/reissue`, {});\n  eq(r2.status, 201, 'Revoked invitation reissues');\n\n  // other pending exists -> 409 when reissuing an older revoked one\n  const r2b = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${first.invitation.id}/reissue`, {});\n  eq([r2b.status, r2b.data?.error?.code ?? r2b.data?.code], [409, 'INVITATION_PENDING'], 'Reissuing an older invitation while another is pending returns 409 INVITATION_PENDING');\n\n  // expired -> reissue\n  const db = new DatabaseSync(harness.database, { timeout: 1000 });\n  db.prepare(\"UPDATE organization_invitations SET expires_at='2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' WHERE id=?\").run(r2.data.invitation.id);\n  db.close();\n  const expiredToken = tokenOf(r2.data.invitePath);\n  const r3 = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${r2.data.invitation.id}/reissue`, {});\n  eq(r3.status, 201, 'Expired invitation reissues');\n  eq((await blake.request('POST', '/api/invitations/inspect', { token: expiredToken })).status, 404, 'Expired link still fails');\n\n  // non-owner denied\n  const casey = new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await casey.signUp('Casey Op', 'casey@reissue.example');\n  const cInv = (await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations`, { email: 'casey@reissue.example' })).data;\n  await casey.request('POST', '/api/invitations/accept', { token: tokenOf(cInv.invitePath) });\n  eq((await casey.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${r3.data.invitation.id}/reissue`, {})).status, 403, 'Operator cannot reissue');\n\n  // accepted -> 409\n  await blake.request('POST', '/api/invitations/accept', { token: tokenOf(r3.data.invitePath) });\n  const r4 = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${r3.data.invitation.id}/reissue`, {});\n  eq([r4.status, r4.data?.error?.code ?? r4.data?.code], [409, 'INVITATION_ACCEPTED'], 'Accepted invitation cannot be reissued');\n\n  // unknown id -> 404\n  eq((await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000/reissue`, {})).status, 404, 'Unknown invitation returns 404');\n\n  // events recorded\n  const edb = new DatabaseSync(harness.database, { timeout: 1000 });\n  const events = edb.prepare(\"SELECT event_type, invitation_id FROM access_events WHERE organization_id=? AND event_type LIKE 'invitation.%' ORDER BY rowid\").all(ws.id).map(e => `${e.event_type}:${e.invitation_id === first.invitation.id ? 'first' : e.invitation_id === r1.data.invitation.id ? 'r1' : e.invitation_id === r2.data.invitation.id ? 'r2' : e.invitation_id === r3.data.invitation.id ? 'r3' : 'other'}`);\n  edb.close();\n  console.log(events);\n  eq(events.slice(0, 7), ['invitation.created:first', 'invitation.revoked:first', 'invitation.created:r1', 'invitation.revoked:r1', 'invitation.created:r2', 'invitation.created:r3', 'invitation.created:other'], 'Reissue is recorded as revoked (only when live) + created');\n  console.log(`${checks} checks passed`);\n} finally { await harness.stop?.(); }\n"}} | {"graders":[{"name":"typecheck","result":"not-run"},{"name":"build","result":"not-run"},{"name":"verify:workspace:access:api","result":"not-run"},{"name":"verify:openapi","result":"not-run"},{"name":"lint","result":"not-run"}],"judges":{"convention":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\reissue-check-ba38bc06-5310-4152-bbe8-31238047cc47\\\\check.mjs\","},"business":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\reissue-check-ba38bc06-5310-4152-bbe8-31238047cc47\\\\check.mjs\","},"scope":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\reissue-check-ba38bc06-5310-4152-bbe8-31238047cc47\\\\check.mjs\","}},"exec":{"input":70,"output":22254,"cacheRead":1920832,"cacheWrite":101553,"costUsd":1.6419504000000003,"subagents":0,"models":["claude-opus-5-5"]},"setup":null,"judgeOverhead":{"input":2,"output":318,"cacheRead":649,"cacheWrite":3805,"costUsd":0.0184689},"attemptKind":"scored","attemptClass":"task-failure","setupWallS":19.991} |

outside Layers: scripts/verify-workspace-access-api.mjs","judge":"not-asked","reason":"","designSha256":"15b7b4ab9353933b7318c3287c907686a01ae2e8bfc2709b74eba559352d620f"}} | {"tool":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\HYPEAM~1\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\typefilter-8ac3d695-c0a8-4c7f-b5d4-37be423d70c1\\check.ts","content":"import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';\nimport { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nconst root = process.argv[2];\nconst { migrate, createWorkspace } = await import(`file:///${root}/server/store.ts`);\nconst { accessActivity } = await import(`file:///${root}/server/access.ts`);\n\nconst db = new DatabaseSync(':memory:');\ndb.exec('CREATE TABLE user(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,name TEXT NOT NULL,email TEXT NOT NULL,emailVerified INTEGER NOT NULL,createdAt INTEGER,updatedAt INTEGER,image TEXT)');\nmigrate(db);\nconst ownerUser = randomUUID(), opUser = randomUUID();\ndb.prepare('INSERT INTO user(id,name,email,emailVerified,createdAt,updatedAt) VALUES(?,?,?,0,0,0)').run(ownerUser, 'Avery Owner', 'o@x.example');\ndb.prepare('INSERT INTO user(id,name,email,emailVerified,createdAt,updatedAt) VALUES(?,?,?,0,0,0)').run(opUser, 'Olly Operator', 'p@x.example');\nconst ws = createWorkspace(db, ownerUser, 'Type Filter Co');\nconst owner = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM organization_memberships WHERE user_id=?').get(ownerUser) as { id: string };\nconst op = randomUUID();\ndb.prepare(\"INSERT INTO organization_memberships(id,organization_id,user_id,role,status,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,'operator','active',?)\").run(op, ws.id, opUser, new Date().toISOString());\ndb.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF');\nconst team = randomUUID();\ndb.prepare(\"INSERT INTO organization_teams(id,organization_id,name,revision,created_at) VALUES(?,?,'Fixture Team',1,?)\").run(team, ws.id, new Date().toISOString());\nlet t = 0; const at = () => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1, 0, 0, t++)).toISOString();\nconst access = (type: string, target: string \| null, summary: string) => db.prepare(`INSERT INTO organization_access_events(id,organization_id,actor_membership_id,actor_name,actor_role,event_type,target_membership_id,target_name,summary,created_at)\n  VALUES(?,?,?,'Avery Owner','owner',?,?,?,?,?)`).run(randomUUID(), ws.id, owner.id, type, target, target ? 'Olly Operator' : null, summary, at());\nfor (let i = 1; i <= 25; i++) db.prepare(`INSERT INTO organization_group_events(id,organization_id,actor_membership_id,actor_name,actor_role,team_id,event_type,summary,created_at)\n  VALUES(?,?,?,'Avery Owner','owner',?,'team.created',?,?)`).run(randomUUID(), ws.id, owner.id, team, `Synthetic fixture event ${String(i).padStart(2, '0')}`, at());\nfor (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) access('invitation.revoked', null, `Private invitation ${i} revoked.`);\naccess('member.updated', op, 'Olly Operator: role change one.');\naccess('member.updated', op, 'Olly Operator: role change two.');\naccess('member.updated', null, 'Untargeted member update.');\naccess('invitation.created', null, 'Private invitation created.');\n\nconst q = (s: string) => accessActivity(db, ws.id, ownerUser, new URLSearchParams(s));\nlet checks = 0; const ok = (v: unknown, e: unknown, m: string) => { assert.deepEqual(v, e, m); checks++; console.log('PASS', m); };\n\nconst all = q('');\nok(all.total, 32, 'unfiltered total unchanged (25+3+3+1)');\nconst teams = q('type=team.created'), teams2 = q('type=team.created&page=2');\nok(teams.total, 25, 'type narrows total');\nok(teams.events.length, 20, 'narrowed page 1 bounded');\nok(teams2.events.length, 5, 'narrowed page 2 holds the remainder');\nok([...teams.events, ...teams2.events].every(e => e.type === 'team.created'), true, 'all narrowed events match type across pages');\nconst revoked = q('type=invitation.revoked');\nok(revoked.total, 3, 'invitation.revoked total');\nok(revoked.total, all.events.concat(q('page=2').events).filter(e => e.type === 'invitation.revoked').length, 'narrowed total matches count in unfiltered feed');\nok(q('type=team.created&search=Synthetic%20fixture%20event%2024').total, 1, 'type combines with search');\nok(q('type=invitation.revoked&search=Synthetic').total, 0, 'non-overlapping type+search gives empty');\nconst byMember = q(`type=member.updated&membershipId=${op}`);\nok(byMember.total, 2, 'type combines with membershipId');\nok(byMember.events.every(e => e.type === 'member.updated' && e.target?.id === op), true, 'type+member events match both');\nfor (const bad of ['type=bogus', 'type=', 'type=Invitation.Revoked', 'type=factory.created']) {\n  assert.throws(() => q(bad), (e: any) => e.status === 422 && e.code === 'INVALID_QUERY', bad); checks++; console.log('PASS 422 INVALID_QUERY for', bad);\n}\nassert.throws(() => accessActivity(db, ws.id, opUser, new URLSearchParams('type=team.created')), (e: any) => e.status === 403); checks++; console.log('PASS non-owner still rejected (403)');\nconsole.log(`${checks} ad-hoc checks passed`);\n"}} | {"graders":[{"name":"typecheck","result":"not-run"},{"name":"build","result":"not-run"},{"name":"verify:workspace:access:api","result":"not-run"},{"name":"verify:openapi","result":"not-run"},{"name":"lint","result":"not-run"}],"judges":{"convention":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\typefilter-8ac3d695-c0a8-4c7f-b5d4-37be423d70c1\\\\check.ts\",\"con"},"business":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\typefilter-8ac3d695-c0a8-4c7f-b5d4-37be423d70c1\\\\check.ts\",\"con"},"scope":{"verdict":"UNKNOWN","reason":"not-run: parked: popup Write: {\"file_path\":\"C:\\\\Users\\\\HYPEAM~1\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\Temp\\\\typefilter-8ac3d695-c0a8-4c7f-b5d4-37be423d70c1\\\\check.ts\",\"con"}},"exec":{"input":100,"output":27636,"cacheRead":2872119,"cacheWrite":180748,"costUsd":2.5735278,"subagents":0,"models":["claude-opus-5-5"]},"setup":null,"judgeOverhead":{"input":2,"output":83,"cacheRead":649,"cacheWrite":3438,"costUsd":0.0146509},"attemptKind":"scored","attemptClass":"task-failure","setupWallS":21.032} |

## Report 2026-11-02 -- arm A2 vs A1 (SPRINT-154 T1, TASK-555)

The log reference for a later `## Why` line is this heading: `docs/research/logs/ab-measurements-a2.md` § Report 2026-11-02.

**Verdict:** A2 run VALID on its own 32 slots. Under Amendment 6 item 7 A2 is **failed**: its pooled primary (78.1%) is below
A0's (89.6%), which fails the never-worse-than-A0 floor. Against A1 there is no gain (-3.1 points, interval spans zero) and no
symptom regression; cost is within the ceiling. Owner-signed 2026-11-02. Under EPIC-023 D6, S1 does not ship as a MINOR.

### Run

- Schedule: `evals/ab/schedule-a2.json`, 2 repos x 8 tasks x k = 2 = 32 slots, plugin pinned at S1 (`82990174`, `b8c3b484`),
  under the Amendment 6 freeze (`2026-11-02T00:40:46Z`, sha256 `f6b770f1...`).
- Validity first (item 6): `bun evals/ab/build-slots.ts --log docs/research/logs/ab-measurements-a2.md --schedule
  evals/ab/schedule-a2.json`, then `analyze.ts` on those slots alone: 32/32 slots, 0 infra-lost, no replacement round, **VALID**.
- Comparison (item 7): `analyze.ts --data <S0 slots>,<A2 slots> --schedule evals/ab/schedule.json,evals/ab/schedule-a2.json`,
  128/128 slots, VALID, no audit finding. The S0 figures it prints equal the S0 report's (A0 89.6%, A1 81.2%, gain -8.3%
  [-20.8%, 2.1%]).
- Spend: A2 scored 64.8123 USD; 0 voided, 0 infra.

### Primary endpoint (first pass; success = graders pass and business judge PASS)

| arm | primary | 90% CI | clinic | workdoo |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A0 | 89.6% | [77.1%, 97.9%] | 95.8% | 83.3% |
| A1 | 81.2% | [66.7%, 93.8%] | 95.8% | 66.7% |
| A2 | 78.1% | [65.6%, 90.6%] | 93.8% | 62.5% |

Gain of A2 over A1 (pooled, decides): **-3.1% [-12.5%, 6.3%]**, not "holds" (below +0.10, interval spans zero). A0 floor: A2
**FAILS** (78.1% < 89.6%). Cost: A2's median execution tokens are 0.93x A1's, within 2.0x. pass@k 93.8% (A1 87.5%), pass^k
62.5% (A1 68.8%).

### Symptom measures (pooled; Unknown = not passed; each arm over its own trials)

| measure | A1 | A2 | A2 - A1 | below -5? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| convention | 30/39 (76.9%) | 20/26 (76.9%) | 0.0 | no |
| business | 23/27 (85.2%) | 15/18 (83.3%) | -1.9 | no |
| lostDetail | 8/12 (66.7%) | 6/8 (75.0%) | +8.3 | no |
| scope | 39/48 (81.3%) | 27/32 (84.4%) | +3.1 | no |

Unknown verdicts: A2 6 / 5 / 4 (convention / business / scope), against A1's 10 / 7 / 6.

### Ruling (Amendment 6 item 7)

- ship-eligible: needs the gain to hold, no regression, A2 not below A0, cost within 2.0x -- the gain does not hold and A2 is below A0.
- failed: a symptom regresses, or A2 is below A0, or its cost exceeds 2.0x A1 -- **A2 is below A0. Ruling: failed.**

### Caveats

- **Three A2 slots parked at a popup and are scored** (pilot-seat-limit, reissue-invitation, access-activity-type-filter, all
  workdoo): each wrote a throwaway check script to the OS temp dir, the shape L-272 records for A1 (3 of 48). They count as
  not-passed on every judge. TASK-573 targets them; TASK-574 measures the fix as arm A4a against A2 (EPIC-023 D11).
- The whole gap to A0 sits in workdoo (A2 62.5%, A1 66.7%, A0 83.3%); clinic is within 2.1 points across all three arms.
- k = 2 for A2 against k = 3 for A0 and A1; task means weight each task equally, so the comparison is per task as frozen.
- workdoo-a2's migrated `overview.md` and `requirements.md` end over their caps (158/150, 211/150), recorded as arm output by
  owner ruling (Amendment 6 item 2).
